DoD similarly: prev_delta stored as i128.\n\nNow the index: in-memory Vec<BlockMeta>. Range read: binary search blocks with ts_end >= from and ts_start <= to.\n\nStorage API:\n\n```rust\npub enum LateMode { Reject, Buffer }\npub struct Repo<S: BlockStore> { ... }\nimpl Repo {\n    fn open(store, mode) -> io::Result<Repo>\n    fn append(&mut self, series, points: &[(i64,i64)]) -> AppendReport { accepted, rejected: Vec<(point, reason)> }\n    fn flush(&mut self, series) -> io::Result<()>\n    fn query(&self, series, from, to) -> io::Result<Vec<(i64,i64)>>\n    fn stats(&self, series) -> Stats\n}\n```\n\nAppendReport: in Reject mode, out-of-order/duplicate points counted rejected with reason; accepted ones appended. Files:\n\nCargo.toml:\n```toml\n[package]\nname = \"tsblock\"\nversion = \"0.1.0\"\nedition = \"2021\"\n\n[dependencies]\n```\n\nsrc/codec.rs, src/io.rs, src/storage.rs, src/json.rs, src/http.rs, src/lib.rs, src/main.rs, tests/acceptance.rs, tests/faults.rs.\n\nLet me write codec.rs carefully.\n\n```rust\n//! Block codec: delta-of-delta timestamps + delta values, zigzag varints,\n//! escape marker for deltas that overflow i64.\n\npub const ESCAPE: u64 = u64::MAX;\n\npub fn zigzag_encode(n: i64) -> u64 { ((n << 1) ^ (n >> 63)) as u64 }\npub fn zigzag_decode(z: u64) -> i64 { ((z >> 1) as i64) ^ -((z & 1) as i64) }\n\npub fn write_varint(buf: &mut Vec<u8>, mut v: u64) {\n    loop {\n        let b = (v & 0x7f) as u8;\n        v >>= 7;\n        if v == 0 { buf.push(b); break; }\n        buf.push(b | 0x80);\n    }\n}\n\npub fn read_varint(buf: &[u8], pos: &mut usize) -> Option<u64> {\n    let mut result: u64 = 0;\n    let mut shift = 0u32;\n    loop {\n        let b = *buf.get(*pos)?; *pos += 1;\n        if shift == 63 {\n            // 10th byte: only 1 bit allowed\n            if b > 1 { return None; }\n            result |= (b as u64) << shift;\n            return Some(result);\n        }\n        result |= ((b & 0x7f) as u64) << shift;\n        if b & 0x80 == 0 { return Some(result); }\n        shift += 7;\n    }\n}\n```\n\nTimestamp encode:\n```rust\npub fn encode_timestamps(ts: &[i64]) -> Vec<u8> {\n    let mut out = Vec::new();\n    if ts.is_empty() { return out; }\n    out.extend_from_slice(&ts[0].to_le_bytes());\n    if ts.len() == 1 { return out; }\n    let mut prev_delta: i128 = ts[1] as i128 - ts[0] as i128;\n    write_delta_or_escape(&mut out, prev_delta, ts[1]);\n    for i in 2..ts.len() {\n        let delta = ts[i] as i128 - ts[i-1] as i128;\n        let dod = delta - prev_delta;\n        write_delta_or_escape(&mut out, dod, ts[i]);\n        prev_delta = delta;\n    }\n    out\n}\n\nfn write_delta_or_escape(out: &mut Vec<u8>, d: i128, raw: i64) {\n    if d >= i64::MIN as i128 && d <= i64::MAX as i128 {\n        write_varint(out, zigzag_encode(d as i64));\n    } else {\n        write_varint(out, ESCAPE);\n        out.extend_from_slice(&raw.to_le_bytes());\n    }\n}\n```\n\nWait — ESCAPE = u64::MAX as varint. But zigzag_encode of i64 never produces u64::MAX? zigzag maps i64::MIN → u64::MAX! zigzag(i64::MIN) = (i64::MIN << 1) ^ (i64::MIN >> 63) = 0 ^ -1 = -1 as u64 = u64::MAX. Conflict! So escape marker u64::MAX collides with zigzag(i64::MIN). Need different escape scheme.\n\nOptions: escape = zigzag(i64::MIN) is a legitimate delta value (delta = i64::MIN possible, e.g. from i64::MAX to -1). So I can't use u64::MAX as escape. Alternative: since deltas that overflow i64 are rare, use a different approach: encode delta in i128... varint of zigzag'd i128 needs 128+ bit varints. Simpler: use escape marker followed by 16-byte raw i128 delta? Or: escape marker = u64::MAX, and special-case zigzag(i64::MIN): when delta == i64::MIN, also use escape path. I.e., escape triggers when d == i64::MIN or d out of i64 range. Then decoder: varint == u64::MAX → read raw 8B absolute value. Clean. Document: \"delta == i64::MIN is encoded via escape to keep u64::MAX unambiguous\".\n\nDecode timestamps:\n```rust\npub fn decode_timestamps(buf: &[u8], count: usize) -> Option<Vec<i64>> {\n    if count == 0 { return Some(vec![]); }\n    if buf.len() < 8 { return None; }\n    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(count);\n    let first = i64::from_le_bytes(buf[0..8].try_into().ok()?);\n    out.push(first);\n    let mut pos = 8;\n    if count == 1 { return Some(out) ... } // must also verify pos==len? Allow trailing? Strict: require pos==buf.len() at end.\n    let mut prev_delta: i128 = read_delta_or_escape(buf, &mut pos, &mut out)?;\n    while out.len() < count {\n        let dod = read_delta_or_escape_raw(buf, &mut pos, &mut out)?; \n        ...\n    }\n}\n```\nHmm, escape semantics differ: escape stores the absolute raw value, not delta. So decode step: read varint; if == ESCAPE → read 8B absolute ts → push, recompute prev_delta = ts - prev_ts. Else delta = zigzag_decode → for second point, prev_delta = delta, push(prev + delta); for later: delta = prev_delta + dod; push(prev + delta); prev_delta = delta. All arithmetic in i128, then check result fits i64 — if not, corrupt → None.\n\nValues codec analogous: first raw, then deltas (escape → raw absolute value).\n\nBlock encode/decode with header & CRC:\n\n```rust\npub const FILE_MAGIC: &[u8; 4] = b\"TSB1\";\npub const FILE_HEADER_LEN: usize = 8;\npub const BLOCK_HEADER_LEN: usize = 48;\n\npub struct BlockMeta { pub count: u32, pub ts_start: i64, pub ts_end: i64, pub val_min: i64, pub val_max: i64, pub offset: u64, pub len: u64 }\n\npub fn encode_block(points: &[(i64,i64)]) -> Vec<u8>\npub fn decode_block(block: &[u8]) -> Option<(BlockMeta-ish, Vec<(i64,i64)>)>\npub fn parse_block_header(buf) -> Option<(header, header_len)>\n```\n\nCRC32 IEEE:\n```rust\npub fn crc32(data: &[u8]) -> u32 { table-based or bitwise }\n```\nBitwise is 8 iterations per byte — fine for tests; but for large data in demo, table better. {"parentUuid":"327240b4-8827-44b3-a409-69e425bcc0e6","isSidechain":false,"message":{"content":[{"id":"call_1cf3a28d71b94d55a1666947","input":{"file_path":"/home/admin/Downloads/biaozhul/opp7/a/src/codec.rs","content":"//! Block codec.\n//!\n//! # Payload encoding\n//!\n//! Timestamps (all integers i64):\n//!   ts[0]            : raw, 8 bytes LE\n//!   ts[1]            : delta d0 = ts[1]-ts[0]          -> zigzag varint\n//!   ts[i] (i >= 2)   : delta-of-delta dod = d(i-1)-d(i-2) -> zigzag varint\n//!\n//! Values:\n//!   v[0]             : raw, 8 bytes LE\n//!   v[i] (i >= 1)    : delta dv = v[i]-v[i-1]          -> zigzag varint\n//!\n//! # Overflow handling (explicit)\n//!\n//! All deltas are computed in i128. A delta that does not fit in i64 — or\n//! that equals i64::MIN (whose zigzag image, u64::MAX, is reserved) — is\n//! encoded as the ESCAPE marker (varint u64::MAX) followed by the *absolute*\n//! value (the raw timestamp or raw value) as 8 bytes LE. Decoding mirrors\n//! this and recomputes the running delta from the absolute value. Decoding\n//! also rejects any reconstruction that would leave the i64 range (corrupt\n//! data), returning None instead of wrapping.\n\npub const ESCAPE: u64 = u64::MAX;\n\npub const FILE_MAGIC: &[u8; 4] = b\"TSB1\";\npub const FILE_VERSION: u16 = 1;\npub const FILE_HEADER_LEN: usize = 8;\npub const BLOCK_HEADER_LEN: usize = 48;\n\n/// Metadata of one encoded block; also the in-memory index entry.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub struct BlockMeta {\n    pub count: u32,\n    pub ts_start: i64,\n    pub ts_end: i64,\n    pub val_min: i64,\n    pub val_max: i64,\n    /// Byte offset of the block header within the data file.\n    pub offset: u64,\n    /// Total byte length of the block (header + payloads).\n    pub len: u64,\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- varint\n\npub fn zigzag_encode(n: i64) -> u64 {\n    ((n << 1) ^ (n >> 63)) as u64\n}\n\npub fn zigzag_decode(z: u64) -> i64 {\n    ((z >> 1) as i64) ^ -((z & 1) as i64)\n}\n\npub fn write_varint(buf: &mut Vec<u8>, mut v: u64) {\n    loop {\n        let b = (v & 0x7f) as u8;\n        v >>= 7;\n        if v == 0 {\n            buf.push(b);\n            return;\n        }\n        buf.push(b | 0x80);\n    }\n}\n\npub fn read_varint(buf: &[u8], pos: &mut usize) -> Option<u64> {\n    let mut result: u64 = 0;\n    let mut shift: u32 = 0;\n    loop {\n        let b = *buf.get(*pos)?;\n        *pos += 1;\n        if shift == 63 {\n            // 10th byte: only one payload bit remains in a u64.\n            if b > 1 {\n                return None;\n            }\n            result |= (b as u64) << shift;\n            return Some(result);\n        }\n        result |= ((b & 0x7f) as u64) << shift;\n        if b & 0x80 == 0 {\n            return Some(result);\n        }\n        shift += 7;\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- crc32\n\n/// CRC-32 (IEEE 802.3, polynomial 0xEDB88320), bitwise.\npub fn crc32(data: &[u8]) -> u32 {\n    let mut crc: u32 = 0xFFFF_FFFF;\n    for &byte in data {\n        crc ^= byte as u32;\n        for _ in 0..8 {\n            let mask = (crc & 1).wrapping_neg();\n            crc = (crc >> 1) ^ (0xEDB8_8320 & mask);\n        }\n    }\n    !crc\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- streams\n\nfn write_delta_or_escape(out: &mut Vec<u8>, delta: i128, absolute: i64) {\n    if delta > i64::MIN as i128 && delta <= i64::MAX as i128 {\n        // Note: i64::MIN is excluded on purpose (zigzag(i64::MIN) == ESCAPE).\n        write_varint(out, zigzag_encode(delta as i64));\n    } else {\n        write_varint(out, ESCAPE);\n        out.extend_from_slice(&absolute.to_le_bytes());\n    }\n}\n\n/// Reads one encoded item. On ESCAPE, reads the absolute value, pushes it\n/// and returns the delta recomputed from `prev`. Otherwise decodes a delta\n/// (relative to `prev_delta`), reconstructs and pushes the value, and\n/// returns the new running delta. All arithmetic in i128; overflow => None.\nfn read_item(\n    buf: &[u8],\n    pos: &mut usize,\n    out: &mut Vec<i64>,\n    prev_delta: i128,\n) -> Option<i128> {\n    let code = read_varint(buf, pos)?;\n    let prev = *out.last()?;\n    if code == ESCAPE {\n        let raw = buf.get(*pos..*pos + 8)?;\n        *pos += 8;\n        let v = i64::from_le_bytes(raw.try_into().ok()?);\n        out.push(v);\n        Some(v as i128 - prev as i128)\n    } else {\n        let step = zigzag_decode(code) as i128;\n        let delta = prev_delta + step;\n        let v = prev as i128 + delta;\n        if v < i64::MIN as i128 || v > i64::MAX as i128 {\n            return None; // corrupt stream would leave i64 range\n        }\n        out.push(v as i64);\n        Some(delta)\n    }\n}\n\npub fn encode_timestamps(ts: &[i64]) -> Vec<u8> {\n    let mut out = Vec::new();\n    if ts.is_empty() {\n        return out;\n    }\n    out.extend_from_slice(&ts[0].to_le_bytes());\n    if ts.len() == 1 {\n        return out;\n    }\n    let mut prev_delta: i128 = ts[1] as i128 - ts[0] as i128;\n    write_delta_or_escape(&mut out, prev_delta, ts[1]);\n    for i in 2..ts.len() {\n        let delta = ts[i] as i128 - ts[i - 1] as i128;\n        let dod = delta - prev_delta;\n        write_delta_or_escape(&mut out, dod, ts[i]);\n        prev_delta = delta;\n    }\n    out\n}\n\npub fn decode_timestamps(buf: &[u8], count: usize) -> Option<Vec<i64>> {\n    let mut out = decode_stream(buf, count)?;\n    // timestamps share the generic stream decoder\n    Some(std::mem::take(&mut out))\n}\n\npub fn encode_values(vals: &[i64]) -> Vec<u8> {\n    let mut out = Vec::new();\n    if vals.is_empty() {\n        return out;\n    }\n    out.extend_from_slice(&vals[0].to_le_bytes());\n    let mut prev_delta: i128 = 0;\n    for i in 1..vals.len() {\n        let delta = vals[i] as i128 - vals[i - 1] as i128;\n        // first delta is absolute against an implicit 0 baseline? No:\n        // encode the delta itself; for i == 1 prev_delta is unused.\n        if i == 1 {\n            write_delta_or_escape(&mut out, delta, vals[i]);\n        } else {\n            write_delta_or_escape(&mut out, delta, vals[i]);\n        }\n        prev_delta = delta;\n    }\n    let _ = prev_delta;\n    out\n}\n\npub fn decode_values(buf: &[u8], count: usize) -> Option<Vec<i64>> {\n    decode_stream(buf, count)\n}\n\n/// Generic decoder for both streams: first element raw i64 LE, subsequent\n/// elements either zigzag-varint steps on the running delta (timestamps:\n/// delta-of-delta; values: plain delta with prev_delta == 0 semantics\n/// handled by caller passing count and the stream having been encoded with\n/// matching prev_delta behaviour).\nfn decode_stream(buf: &[u8], count: usize) -> Option<Vec<i64>> {\n    if count == 0 {\n        if buf.is_empty() {\n            return Some(Vec::new());\n        }\n        return None;\n    }\n    let first_raw = buf.get(0..8)?;\n    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(count);\n    out.push(i64::from_le_bytes(first_raw.try_into().ok()?));\n    let mut pos = 8usize;\n    if count >= 2 {\n        // second element: step is the first delta itself (prev_delta = 0)\n        let mut prev_delta = read_item(buf, &mut pos, &mut out, 0)?;\n        while out.len() < count {\n            prev_delta = read_item(buf, &mut pos, &mut out, prev_delta)?;\n        }\n    }\n    if pos != buf.len() {\n        return None; // trailing garbage => corrupt\n    }\n    Some(out)\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- blocks\n\n/// Encodes a full block (header + payloads) for `points`, which must be\n/// non-empty and sorted by strictly increasing timestamp (caller ensures).\npub fn encode_block(points: &[(i64, i64)]) -> Vec<u8> {\n    debug_assert!(!points.is_empty());\n    let ts: Vec<i64> = points.iter().map(|p| p.0).collect();\n    let vals: Vec<i64> = points.iter().map(|p| p.1).collect();\n    let ts_payload = encode_timestamps(&ts);\n    let val_payload = encode_values(&vals);\n\n    let mut crc_input = Vec::with_capacity(ts_payload.len() + val_payload.len());\n    crc_input.extend_from_slice(&ts_payload);\n    crc_input.extend_from_slice(&val_payload);\n    let crc = crc32(&crc_input);\n\n    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(BLOCK_HEADER_LEN + ts_payload.len() + val_payload.len());\n    out.extend_from_slice(&(points.len() as u32).to_le_bytes());\n    out.extend_from_slice(&ts[0].to_le_bytes());\n    out.extend_from_slice(&ts[ts.len() - 1].to_le_bytes());\n    out.extend_from_slice(&vals.iter().min().unwrap().to_le_bytes());\n    out.extend_from_slice(&vals.iter().max().unwrap().to_le_bytes());\n    out.extend_from_slice(&(ts_payload.len() as u32).to_le_bytes());\n    out.extend_from_slice(&(val_payload.len() as u32).to_le_bytes());\n    out.extend_from_slice(&crc.to_le_bytes());\n    debug_assert_eq!(out.len(), BLOCK_HEADER_LEN);\n    out.extend_from_slice(&ts_payload);\n    out.extend_from_slice(&val_payload);\n    out\n}\n\npub struct BlockHeader {\n    pub count: u32,\n    pub ts_start: i64,\n    pub ts_end: i64,\n    pub val_min: i64,\n    pub val_max: i64,\n    pub ts_len: u32,\n    pub val_len: u32,\n    pub crc32: u32,\n}\n\npub fn parse_block_header(buf: &[u8]) -> Option<BlockHeader> {\n    if buf.len() < BLOCK_HEADER_LEN {\n        return None;\n    }\n    Some(BlockHeader {\n        count: u32::from_le_bytes(buf[0..4].try_into().ok()?),\n        ts_start: i64::from_le_bytes(buf[4..12].try_into().ok()?),\n        ts_end: i64::from_le_bytes(buf[12..20].try_into().ok()?),\n        val_min: i64::from_le_bytes(buf[20..28].try_into().ok()?),\n        val_max: i64::from_le_bytes(buf[28..36].try_into().ok()?),\n        ts_len: u32::from_le_bytes(buf[36..40].try_into().ok()?),\n        val_len: u32::from_le_bytes(buf[40..44].try_into().ok()?),\n        crc32: u32::from_le_bytes(buf[44..48].try_into().ok()?),\n    })\n}\n\n/// Decodes a complete block (header + payloads). Verifies CRC and that the\n/// decoded point count matches the header. Block codec.\n//!\n//! # Payload encoding\n//!\n//! Timestamps (all integers i64):\n//!   ts[0]            : raw, 8 bytes LE\n//!   ts[1]            : delta d0 = ts[1]-ts[0]          -> zigzag varint\n//!   ts[i] (i >= 2)   : delta-of-delta dod = d(i-1)-d(i-2) -> zigzag varint\n//!\n//! Values:\n//!   v[0]             : raw, 8 bytes LE\n//!   v[i] (i >= 1)    : delta dv = v[i]-v[i-1]          -> zigzag varint\n//!\n//! # Overflow handling (explicit)\n//!\n//! All deltas are computed in i128. A delta that does not fit in i64 — or\n//! that equals i64::MIN (whose zigzag image, u64::MAX, is reserved) — is\n//! encoded as the ESCAPE marker (varint u64::MAX) followed by the *absolute*\n//! value (the raw timestamp or raw value) as 8 bytes LE. Decoding mirrors\n//! this and recomputes the running delta from the absolute value. Decoding\n//! also rejects any reconstruction that would leave the i64 range (corrupt\n//! data), returning None instead of wrapping.\n\npub const ESCAPE: u64 = u64::MAX;\n\npub const FILE_MAGIC: &[u8; 4] = b\"TSB1\";\npub const FILE_VERSION: u16 = 1;\npub const FILE_HEADER_LEN: usize = 8;\npub const BLOCK_HEADER_LEN: usize = 48;\n\n/// Metadata of one encoded block; also the in-memory index entry.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub struct BlockMeta {\n    pub count: u32,\n    pub ts_start: i64,\n    pub ts_end: i64,\n    pub val_min: i64,\n    pub val_max: i64,\n    /// Byte offset of the block header within the data file.\n    pub offset: u64,\n    /// Total byte length of the block (header + payloads).\n    pub len: u64,\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- varint\n\npub fn zigzag_encode(n: i64) -> u64 {\n    ((n << 1) ^ (n >> 63)) as u64\n}\n\npub fn zigzag_decode(z: u64) -> i64 {\n    ((z >> 1) as i64) ^ -((z & 1) as i64)\n}\n\npub fn write_varint(buf: &mut Vec<u8>, mut v: u64) {\n    loop {\n        let b = (v & 0x7f) as u8;\n        v >>= 7;\n        if v == 0 {\n            buf.push(b);\n            return;\n        }\n        buf.push(b | 0x80);\n    }\n}\n\npub fn read_varint(buf: &[u8], pos: &mut usize) -> Option<u64> {\n    let mut result: u64 = 0;\n    let mut shift: u32 = 0;\n    loop {\n        let b = *buf.get(*pos)?;\n        *pos += 1;\n        if shift == 63 {\n            // 10th byte: only one payload bit remains in a u64.\n            if b > 1 {\n                return None;\n            }\n            result |= (b as u64) << shift;\n            return Some(result);\n        }\n        result |= ((b & 0x7f) as u64) << shift;\n        if b & 0x80 == 0 {\n            return Some(result);\n        }\n        shift += 7;\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- crc32\n\n/// CRC-32 (IEEE 802.3, polynomial 0xEDB88320), bitwise.\npub fn crc32(data: &[u8]) -> u32 {\n    let mut crc: u32 = 0xFFFF_FFFF;\n    for &byte in data {\n        crc ^= byte as u32;\n        for _ in 0..8 {\n            let mask = (crc & 1).wrapping_neg();\n            crc = (crc >> 1) ^ (0xEDB8_8320 & mask);\n        }\n    }\n    !crc\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- streams\n\nfn write_delta_or_escape(out: &mut Vec<u8>, delta: i128, absolute: i64) {\n    if delta > i64::MIN as i128 && delta <= i64::MAX as i128 {\n        // Note: i64::MIN is excluded on purpose (zigzag(i64::MIN) == ESCAPE).\n        write_varint(out, zigzag_encode(delta as i64));\n    } else {\n        write_varint(out, ESCAPE);\n        out.extend_from_slice(&absolute.to_le_bytes());\n    }\n}\n\n/// Reads one encoded item. On ESCAPE, reads the absolute value, pushes it\n/// and returns the delta recomputed from `prev`. Otherwise decodes a delta\n/// (relative to `prev_delta`), reconstructs and pushes the value, and\n/// returns the new running delta. All arithmetic in i128; overflow => None.\nfn read_item(\n    buf: &[u8],\n    pos: &mut usize,\n    out: &mut Vec<i64>,\n    prev_delta: i128,\n) -> Option<i128> {\n    let code = read_varint(buf, pos)?;\n    let prev = *out.last()?;\n    if code == ESCAPE {\n        let raw = buf.get(*pos..*pos + 8)?;\n        *pos += 8;\n        let v = i64::from_le_bytes(raw.try_into().ok()?);\n        out.push(v);\n        Some(v as i128 - prev as i128)\n    } else {\n        let step = zigzag_decode(code) as i128;\n        let delta = prev_delta + step;\n        let v = prev as i128 + delta;\n        if v < i64::MIN as i128 || v > i64::MAX as i128 {\n            return None; // corrupt stream would leave i64 range\n        }\n        out.push(v as i64);\n        Some(delta)\n    }\n}\n\npub fn encode_timestamps(ts: &[i64]) -> Vec<u8> {\n    let mut out = Vec::new();\n    if ts.is_empty() {\n        return out;\n    }\n    out.extend_from_slice(&ts[0].to_le_bytes());\n    if ts.len() == 1 {\n        return out;\n    }\n    let mut prev_delta: i128 = ts[1] as i128 - ts[0] as i128;\n    write_delta_or_escape(&mut out, prev_delta, ts[1]);\n    for i in 2..ts.len() {\n        let delta = ts[i] as i128 - ts[i - 1] as i128;\n        let dod = delta - prev_delta;\n        write_delta_or_escape(&mut out, dod, ts[i]);\n        prev_delta = delta;\n    }\n    out\n}\n\npub fn decode_timestamps(buf: &[u8], count: usize) -> Option<Vec<i64>> {\n    let mut out = decode_stream(buf, count)?;\n    // timestamps share the generic stream decoder\n    Some(std::mem::take(&mut out))\n}\n\npub fn encode_values(vals: &[i64]) -> Vec<u8> {\n    let mut out = Vec::new();\n    if vals.is_empty() {\n        return out;\n    }\n    out.extend_from_slice(&vals[0].to_le_bytes());\n    let mut prev_delta: i128 = 0;\n    for i in 1..vals.len() {\n        let delta = vals[i] as i128 - vals[i - 1] as i128;\n        // first delta is absolute against an implicit 0 baseline? write_delta_or_escape(&mut out, delta, vals[i]);\n        }\n        prev_delta = delta;\n    }\n    let _ = prev_delta;\n    out\n}\n\npub fn decode_values(buf: &[u8], count: usize) -> behaviour).\nfn decode_stream(buf: &[u8], count: usize) -> Option<Vec<i64>> {\n    if count == 0 {\n        if buf.is_empty() {\n            return Some(Vec::new());\n        }\n        return None;\n    }\n    let first_raw = buf.get(0..8)?;\n    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(count);\n    out.push(i64::from_le_bytes(first_raw.try_into().ok()?));\n    let mut pos = 8usize;\n    if count >= 2 {\n out.extend_from_slice(&val_payload);\n    out\n}\n\npub struct BlockHeader {\n    pub count: u32,\n    pub ts_start: i64,\n    pub ts_end: i64,\n    pub val_min: i64,\n    pub val_max: i64,\n    pub All arithmetic in i128; overflow => None.\nfn read_item(\n    buf: &[u8],\n    pos: &mut usize,\n    out: &mut Vec<i64>,\n    prev_delta: i128,\n) -> Option<i128> {\n    let code = read_varint(buf, pos)?;\n    let prev = *out.last()?;\n    if code == ESCAPE {\n        let raw = buf.get(*pos..*pos + 8)?;\n        *pos += 8;\n        let v = i64::from_le_bytes(raw.try_into().ok()?);\n        out.push(v);\n        Some(v as i128 - prev as i128)\n    } else {\n        let step = zigzag_decode(code) as i128;\n        let delta = prev_delta + step;\n        let v = prev as i128 + delta;\n        if v < i64::MIN as i128 || v > i64::MAX as i128 {\n            return None; // corrupt stream would leave i64 range\n        }\n        out.push(v as i64);\n        Some(delta)\n    }\n}","new_string":"/// Reads one timestamp item. On ESCAPE, reads the absolute timestamp, pushes\n/// it and returns the delta recomputed from the previous timestamp.\n/// Otherwise decodes a delta-of-delta step, reconstructs and pushes the\n/// timestamp, and returns the new running delta. All arithmetic in i128;\n/// leaving the i64 range => None (corrupt stream).\nfn read_ts_item(\n    buf: &[u8],\n    pos: &mut usize,\n    out: &mut Vec<i64>,\n    prev_delta: i128,\n) -> Option<i128> {\n    let code = read_varint(buf, pos)?;\n    let prev = *out.last()?;\n    if code == ESCAPE {\n        let raw = buf.get(*pos..*pos + 8)?;\n        *pos += 8;\n        let v = i64::from_le_bytes(raw.try_into().ok()?);\n        out.push(v);\n        Some(v as i128 - prev as i128)\n    } else {\n        let dod = zigzag_decode(code) as i128;\n        let delta = prev_delta + dod;\n        let v = prev as i128 + delta;\n        if v < i64::MIN as i128 || v > i64::MAX as i128 {\n            return None;\n        }\n        out.push(v as i64);\n        Some(delta)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Reads one value item. Injectable file I/O layer.\n//!\n//! All persistence in the repository goes through the [`FileIO`] trait, so\n//! tests can substitute [`MockIO`] to inject failures (append/sync/read\n//! errors, torn writes, bit corruption) without touching real disks.\n//!\n//! std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\n\npub trait FileIO {\n    /// Appends `data` to the file, creating it (and parent dirs) if needed.\n    /// Returns the offset at which the data was written.\n    fn append(&mut self, path: &str, data: &[u8]) -> io::Result<u64>;\n    /// Reads the whole file; a missing file yields `Ok(vec![])`.\n    fn read_all(&self, path: &str) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>>;\n    /// bytes are durable.\n    fn sync(&mut self, path: &str) -> io::Result<()>;\n    /// Truncates the file to `len` bytes (used by crash recovery).\n    fn truncate(&mut self, path: &str, len: u64) -> io::Result<()>;\n    /// Current file length in bytes; missing file => 0.\n    fn len(&self, path: &str) -> io::Result<u64>;\n    /// Lists logical paths under `prefix` (one path component deep).\n    fn list(&self, prefix: &str) -> io::Result<Vec<String>>;\n}\n\n// ------------------------------------------------------------------ FsIO\n\n/// Real filesystem-backed I/O rooted at a directory.\npub struct FsIO {\n    root: PathBuf,\n}\n\nimpl FsIO {\n    pub fn new(root: impl Into<PathBuf>) -> Self {\n        FsIO { root: root.into() }\n    }\n\n    fn resolve(&self, path: &str) -> PathBuf {\n        // Logical paths are built internally from validated series names\n        // ([A-Za-z0-9_-] only), so no traversal is possible.\n        self.root.join(path)\n    }\n}\n\nimpl FileIO for FsIO {\n    fn append(&mut self, path: &str, data: &[u8]) -> io::Result<u64> {\n        let p = self.resolve(path);\n        if let Some(dir) = p.parent() {\n            std::fs::create_dir_all(dir)?;\n        }\n        let mut f = OpenOptions::new().create(true).append(true).open(&p)?;\n        let offset = f.metadata()?.len();\n        f.write_all(data)?;\n        Ok(offset)\n    }\n\n    fn read_all(&self, path: &str) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>> {\n        match File::open(self.resolve(path)) {\n            Ok(mut f) => {\n                let mut buf = Vec::new();\n                f.read_to_end(&mut buf)?;\n                Ok(buf)\n            }\n            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(Vec::new()),\n            Err(e) => Err(e),\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn read_at(&self, path: &str, offset: u64, len: usize) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>> {\n        let mut f = File::open(self.resolve(path))?;\n        f.seek(SeekFrom::Start(offset))?;\n        let mut buf = vec![0u8; len];\n        f.read_exact(&mut buf)?;\n        Ok(buf)\n    }\n\n    fn sync(&mut self, path: &str) -> io::Result<()> {\n        let p = self.resolve(path);\n        if !Path::new(&p).exists() {\n            return Ok(());\n        }\n        File::open(p)?.sync_all()\n    }\n\n    fn truncate(&mut self, path: &str, len: u64) -> io::Result<()> {\n        let f = OpenOptions::new().write(true).open(self.resolve(path))?;\n        f.set_len(len)\n    }\n\n    fn len(&self, path: &str) -> io::Result<u64> {\n        match std::fs::metadata(self.resolve(path)) {\n            Ok(m) => Ok(m.len()),\n            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(0),\n            Err(e) => Err(e),\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn list(&self, prefix: &str) -> io::Result<Vec<String>> {\n        let dir = self.resolve(prefix);\n        let mut out = Vec::new();\n        let entries = match std::fs::read_dir(&dir) {\n            Ok(e) => e,\n            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(out),\n            Err(e) => return Err(e),\n        };\n        for entry in entries {\n            let entry = entry?;\n            if let Some(name) = entry.file_name().to_str() {\n                out.push(format!(\"{}/{}\", prefix.trim_end_matches('/'), name));\n            }\n        }\n        out.sort();\n        Ok(out)\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- MockIO\n\n/// In-memory I/O with fault injection, for tests.\n#[derive(Default)]\npub struct MockIO {\n    pub files: /// to a matching path: (path substring, offset from file start, mask).\n    pub corrupt_on_append: Option<(String, u64, u8)>,\n    appends: u64,\n}\n\nimpl MockIO {\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self::default()\n    }\n\n    fn failing(&self) -> io::Error {\n        io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::Other, \"injected fault\")\n    }\n}\n\nimpl FileIO for MockIO {\n    fn append(&mut self, path: &str, data: &[u8]) -> io::Result<u64> {\n        self.appends += 1;\n        if let Some(limit) = self.fail_appends_after {\n            if self.appends > limit {\n                return Err(self.failing());\n            }\n        }\n        let file = self.files.entry(path.to_string()).or_default();\n        let offset = file.len() as u64;\n        file.extend_from_slice(data);\n mask;\n            }\n        }\n        Ok(offset)\n    }\n\n    fn read_all(&self, path: &str) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>> {\n        if self.fail_reads_on.iter().any(|p| path.contains(p)) {\n            return Err(self.failing());\n        }\n        Ok(self.files.get(path).cloned().unwrap_or_default())\n    }\n\n    fn read_at(&self, path: &str, offset: u64, len: usize) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>> {\n start = offset as usize;\n        let end = start + len;\n        if end > file.len() {\n            return Err(io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::UnexpectedEof, \"short read\"));\n        }\n        Ok(file[start..end].to_vec())\n    }\n\n    fn sync(&mut self, path: &str) -> io::Result<()> {\n        if self.fail_sync_on.iter().any(|p| path.contains(p)) {\n            return Err(self.failing());\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    fn truncate(&mut self, path: &str, len: u64) -> io::Result<()> {\n        let file = self.files.entry(path.to_string()).or_default();\n list(&self, prefix: &str) -> io::Result<Vec<String>> {\n        let p = format!(\"{}/\", prefix.trim_end_matches('/'));\n        Ok(self\n            .files\n            .keys()\n File-backed time-series repository.\n//!\n//! # On-disk layout\n//!\n//! Per series, two files under the storage root:\n//!\n//! ```text\n//! data/<series>.tsb   -- block file\n//!   [0..8)   file header: magic \"TSB1\", version u16 LE (=1), reserved u16\n//!   then a sequence of blocks:\n//!     block header (48 bytes):\n//!       count    u32 LE   number of points\n//!       ts_start i64 LE   first timestamp\n//!       ts_end   i64 LE   last timestamp\n//!       val_min  i64 LE\n//!       val_max  i64 LE\n//!       ts_len   u32 LE   timestamp payload bytes\n//!       val_len  u32 LE   value payload bytes\n//!       crc32    u32 LE   CRC-32/IEEE over (ts_payload ++ val_payload)\n//!     ts_payload  (ts_len bytes)   delta-of-delta, see codec\n//!     val_payload (val_len bytes)  plain delta, see codec\n//!\n//! late/<series>.tsl   -- late/out-of-order log (only in Buffer mode)\n//!   raw records, 16 bytes each: ts i64 LE, val i64 LE, append order\n//! ```\n//!\n//! The block index is NOT persisted; it is rebuilt on open by scanning block\n//! headers and verifying CRCs (see \"Sync boundaries\").\n//!\n//! # Sync boundaries\n//!\n//! * Points accepted by `append` live only in memory until `flush`.\n//! * `flush` encodes pending points into one block, appends it to the data\n//!   file, then calls `sync` (fsync) on the data file (and the late log if\n//!   non-empty). After `flush` returns Ok, those points are durable.\n//! * A crash may leave a torn tail (partial block / half-written header).\n//!   On `open`, the file is scanned block by block; the first header that is\n//!   incomplete, has impossible lengths, or fails CRC terminates the scan\n//!   and the file is truncated to that offset. All blocks before it are\n//!   valid and indexed. A late log whose length is not a multiple of 16 is\n//!   truncated to a 16-byte boundary.\n//! * After a `flush` error (injected or real I/O failure), in-memory state\n//!   may diverge from disk; callers must drop the repo and `open` again\n//!   (recovery path) before retrying.\n//!\n//! # Ordering semantics\n//!\n//! Timestamps within a series must be strictly increasing.\n//! * `LateMode::Reject` (default): a point with ts <= last accepted ts is\n//!   rejected (`duplicate` when ts == last, `out_of_order` when ts < last).\n//! * `LateMode::Buffer`: such points are appended to the series' late log.\n//!   At query time the late log is merged with the main blocks; on\n//!   timestamp collision the point from the main (in-order) stream wins,\n//!   and within the late log the earliest appended record wins\n//!   (first-write-wins). Late points never modify flushed blocks.\n\nuse crate::codec::{self, BlockMeta};\nuse crate::io::FileIO;\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\nuse std::io;\n\npub const DEFAULT_BLOCK_SIZE: usize = b'-')\n}\n\nimpl<IO: FileIO> Repo<IO> {\n    /// Opens (or creates) a repository over `io`, rebuilding all in-memory\n    /// indexes from disk and truncating torn tails (see module docs).\n    pub fn open(mut io: IO, mode: LateMode, block_size: usize) -> io::Result<Self> {\n        let mut repo = Repo {\n            io,\n            mode,\n            block_size: block_size.max(1),\n the in-memory index.\n    fn recover_series(&mut self, name: &str) -> io::Result<()> {\n        let data = self.io.read_all(&data_path(name))?;\n        let mut state = SeriesState::default();\n        if data.is_empty() {\n            self.series.insert(name.to_string(), state);\n            return Ok(());\n        }\n        if data.len() < codec::FILE_HEADER_LEN || &data[0..4] != codec::FILE_MAGIC {\n            return Err(io::Error::new(\n                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,\n                format!(\"series {}: bad file header\", name),\n            ));\n        }\n        let mut pos = codec::FILE_HEADER_LEN;\n        while pos < data.len() {\n            let header_end = pos + codec::BLOCK_HEADER_LEN;\n            let header = match data.get(pos..header_end).and_then(codec::parse_block_header) {\n                Some(h) => h,\n                None => break, // torn header\n            };\n            let block_len = self.ensure_series(name)?;\n        let mut outcome = AppendOutcome::default();\n        let mut late_records: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();\n        {\n            let state = self.series.get_mut(name).unwrap();\n            let mut last_ts = state.last_ts;\n            for &(ts, val) in points {\n                match last_ts {\n                    None => {\n last => {\n                        state.pending.push((ts, val));\n                        last_ts = Some(ts);\n                        outcome.accepted += 1;\n                    }\n LateMode::Reject => {\n                                outcome.rejected.push(Rejected { ts, val, reason });\n                            }\n                            LateMode::Buffer => {\n                                late_records.extend_from_slice(&ts.to_le_bytes());\n                                late_records.extend_from_slice(&val.to_le_bytes());\n                                state.late_points += 1;\n                                outcome.buffered += 1;\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            state.last_ts = last_ts;\n        }\n        if !late_records.is_empty() {\n            self.io.append(&late_path(name), &late_records)?;\n        }\n        Ok(outcome)\n    }\n\n    /// {\n        self.ensure_series(name)?;\n        let pending = std::mem::take(&mut self.series.get_mut(name).unwrap().pending);\n        if !pending.is_empty() {\n            for chunk in pending.chunks(self.block_size) {\n                let block = codec::encode_block(chunk);\n                let h = codec::parse_block_header(&block).expect(\"own block header\");\n                let offset = self.io.append(&data_path(name), &block)?;\n                self.series\n                    .get_mut(name)\n                    .unwrap()\n                    .index\n                    .push(BlockMeta {\n                        count: h.count,\n                        ts_start: h.ts_start,\n                        ts_end: h.ts_end,\n                        val_min: h.val_min,\n                        val_max: h.val_max,\n                        offset,\n                        len: block.len() as u64,\n                    });\n            }\n        }\n        self.io.sync(&data_path(name))?;\n        if self.series[name].late_points > 0 {\n            self.io.sync(&late_path(name))?;\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Range read, inclusive bounds: all points with `from <= ts <= to`.\n    /// In Buffer mode, late-log points are merged in (main stream wins on\n    /// timestamp collision).\n    pub fn query(&self, name: &str, from: i64, to: i64) -> io::Result<Vec<(i64, i64)>> {\n        if from > to {\n            return Err(io::Error::new(\n                io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,\n                \"query range: from > to\",\n            ));\n        }\n        let state = match self.series.get(name) {\n            Some(s) => s,\n            None => return Ok(Vec::new()),\n        };\n        let mut out: Vec<(i64, i64)> =               .read_at(&data_path(name), meta.offset, meta.len as usize)?;\n            let points = codec::decode_block(&raw).ok_or_else(|| {\n                io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidData, \"block failed verification\")\n            })?;\n            out.extend(points.into_iter().filter(|p| p.0 >= from && p.0 <= to));\n        }\n        // pending (unflushed) points are visible to reads in this process\n        out.extend(state.pending.iter().copied().filter(|p| p.0 >= from && p.0 <= to));\n\n        if self.mode == LateMode::Buffer && to).collect();\n            late_in_range.sort_by_key(|p| p.0);\n            // first-write-wins within the late log\n            late_in_range.dedup_by_key(|p| p.0);\n            // main stream wins on collision\n            let main_ts: std::collections::HashSet<i64> = out.iter().map(|p| p.0).collect();\n            late_in_range.retain(|p| !main_ts.contains(&p.0));\n            out.extend(late_in_range);\n            out.sort_by_key(|p| p.0);\n        }\n        Ok(out)\n    }\n\n    fn read_late(&self, name: &str) -> io::Result<Vec<(i64, i64)>> {\n        let raw = i64::from_le_bytes(rec[0..8].try_into().unwrap());\n            let val = i64::from_le_bytes(rec[8..16].try_into().unwrap());\n            out.push((ts, val));\n        }\n        Ok(out)\n    }\n\n data.get(pos..header_end).and_then(codec::parse_block_header) {\n                Some(h) => h,\n                None => break, // torn header\n            };\n            let block_len = codec::BLOCK_HEADER_LEN\n                .saturating_add(header.ts_len as usize)\n                .saturating_add(header.val_len as usize);\n            let end = match pos.checked_add(block_len) {\n                Some(e) if e <= data.len() => e,\n                _ => break, // torn payload or absurd lengths\n            };\n            if header.count == 0 || codec::decode_block(&data[pos..end]).is_none() {\n                break; // CRC failure / corrupt payload\n            }\n            state.index.push(BlockMeta {\n                count: {\n        self.ensure_series(name)?;\n        let pending = std::mem::take(&mut self.series.get_mut(name).unwrap().pending);\n        if !pending.is_empty() {\n            for chunk in 64; // small blocks to exercise block boundaries\n\nfn test_dir(name: &str) -> PathBuf {\n    let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(\"tsblock-acc-{}-{}\", std::process::id(), name));\n    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);\n    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();\n    dir\n}\n\nfn open(dir: &PathBuf, mode: LateMode) -> Repo<FsIO> {\n    Repo::open(FsIO::new(dir), mode, BLOCK).unwrap()\n}\n\n/// Reference filter: inclusive range over the uncompressed array.\nfn reference_query(data: &[(i64, i64)], from: i64, to: i64) -> Vec<(i64, i64)> {\n    data.iter()\n        .copied()\n        .filter(|p| p.0 >= from && p.0 <= to)\n        .collect()\n}\n\nfn regular_dataset(n: i64) -> Vec<(i64, i64)> {\n    // 1-second cadence, values crossing zero (negatives included)\n    (0..n)\n        .map(|i| (1_700_000_000 + i, ((i * 37) % 1001) - 500))\n        .collect()\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn full_and_boundary_queries_match_reference() {\n    let dir = test_dir(\"boundary\");\n    let data = regular_dataset(1000);\n\n    let mut repo = open(&dir, LateMode::Reject);\n    let outcome = repo.append(\"cpu\", &data).unwrap();\n    assert_eq!(outcome.accepted, data.len());\n    repo.flush(\"cpu\").unwrap();\n\n    // block boundaries: with BLOCK=64, blocks start at ts offsets 0,64,128,...\n    let base = data[0].0;\n    let cases: Vec<(i64, i64)> = vec![\n        (base, base + 999),                 // full range\n        (base, base),                       // single first point\n        (base + 999, base + 999),           // single last point\n        (base + 64, base + 127),            // exactly one block\n        (base + 63, base + 64),             // straddling a boundary\n        (base + 64, base + 64),             // first point of a block\n        (base + 127, base + 128),           // last of block + first of next\n        (base - 1000, base - 1),            // entirely before\n        (base + 1000, base + 2000),         // entirely after\n        (base + 500, base + 499),           // (invalid, tested separately)\n    ];\n    for &(from, to) in &cases[..9] {\n        let got = repo.query(\"cpu\", from, to).unwrap();\n        let want = reference_query(&data, from, to);\n        assert_eq!(got, want, \"range [{}, {}]\", from, to);\n    }\n    // invalid range must error\n    assert!(repo.query(\"cpu\", base + 500, base + 499).is_err());\n    // unknown series => empty\n    assert_eq!(repo.query(\"nope\", 0, i64::MAX).unwrap(), vec![]);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn negative_values_and_zero_crossing() {\n    let dir = test_dir(\"negatives\");\n    let data: Vec<(i64, i64)> = vec![\n        (10, -500),\n        (20, -1),\n        (30, 0),\n        (40, 1),\n        (50, -9_000_000_000),\n        (60, allow it, fail the block append\n    io.fail_appends_after = Some(1);\n    let mut repo = open(io);\n    repo.append(\"s\", &points(0, 5)).unwrap();\n    assert!(repo.flush(\"s\").is_err());\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn torn_tail_is_truncated_on_recovery() {\n    // write two blocks, then simulate a crash mid-third-block by truncating\n    // the file at half of the third block\n    let mut repo = open(MockIO::new());\n    repo.append(\"s\", &points(0, 8)).unwrap();\n    repo.flush(\"s\").unwrap();\n    repo.append(\"s\", &points(8, 8)).unwrap();\n    repo.flush(\"s\").unwrap();\n    repo.append(\"s\", &points(16, 8)).unwrap();\n    repo.flush(\"s\").unwrap();\n\n    let mut io = repo.into_io();\n    let len = io.len(\"data/s.tsb\").unwrap();\n    // find start of third block: scan is done by recovery; emulate crash by\n    // chopping 7 bytes off the end (inside the last block's payload)\n    io.truncate(\"data/s.tsb\", len - 7).unwrap();\n\n    let repo = open(io);\n    // the torn block is dropped; the first two blocks are intact\n    assert_eq!(repo.query(\"s\", 0, 1000).unwrap(), points(0, 16));\n    let stats = repo.stats(\"s\").unwrap();\n    assert_eq!(stats.blocks, 2);\n    assert_eq!(stats.points, 16);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn corrupt_block_crc_drops_only_that_block() {\n    let mut repo = open(MockIO::new());\n    repo.append(\"s\", &points(0, 8)).unwrap();\n    repo.flush(\"s\").unwrap();\n    repo.append(\"s\", &points(8, 8)).unwrap();\n    repo.flush(\"s\").unwrap();\n\n    let mut io = repo.into_io();\n    // flip one byte inside the second block's payload\n    let len = io.len(\"data/s.tsb\").unwrap();\n    let mut data = io.read_all(\"data/s.tsb\").unwrap();\n    let pos = (len - 3) as usize;\n    data[pos] ^= 0x5A;\n    io.files.insert(\"data/s.tsb\".to_string(), data);\n\n    let repo = open(io);\n    assert_eq!(repo.query(\"s\", 0, 1000).unwrap(), points(0, 8));\n    assert_eq!(repo.stats(\"s\").unwrap().blocks, 1);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn read_failure_surfaces_on_query() {\n    let mut repo = open(MockIO::new());\n    repo.append(\"s\", &points(0, 8)).unwrap();\n    repo.flush(\"s\").unwrap();\n    let mut io = repo.into_io();\n    io.fail_reads_on.push(\"data/\".to_string());\n    let repo = Repo {\n        // reopen would fail on read; construct via open on a clone instead\n        ..open(MockIO::new())\n    };\n    drop(repo);\n    // simpler: build repo over failing io directly\n    let mut io2 = MockIO::new();\n    io2.files.insert(\n        \"data/s.tsb\".to_string(),\n        io.files.get(\"data/s.tsb\").unwrap().clone(),\n    );\n    let repo = open(io2);\n    let mut io3 = repo.into_io();\n    io3.fail_reads_on.push(\"data/\".to_string());\n    // reopening with failing reads fails at recovery time\n    assert!(Repo::open(io3, LateMode::Reject, BLOCK).is_err());\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn corrupt_late_log_tail_is_truncated() {\n    let mut repo = Repo::open(MockIO::new(), LateMode::Buffer, BLOCK).unwrap();\n    repo.append(\"s\", &[(100, 1), (50, 0)]).unwrap(); // (50,0) -> late log\n    repo.flush(\"s\").unwrap();\n    let mut io = repo.into_io();\n    // append 5 stray bytes to the late log (torn record)\n    io.append(\"late/s.tsl\", &[1, 2, 3, 4, 5]).unwrap();\n    let repo = Repo::open(io, LateMode::Buffer, BLOCK).unwrap();\n    assert_eq!(\n        repo.query(\"s\", 0, 1000).unwrap(),\n        vec![(50, 0), (100, 1)]\n    );\n    assert_eq!(repo.stats(\"s\").unwrap().late_points, 1);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn recovery_allows_continued_writes() {\n    let mut repo = open(MockIO::new());\n    repo.append(\"s\", &points(0, 8)).unwrap();\n    repo.flush(\"s\").unwrap();\n    let mut io = repo.into_io();\n    let len = io.len(\"data/s.tsb\").unwrap();\n    io.append(\"data/s.tsb\", &[0xDE, 0xAD, 0xBE]).unwrap(); // torn tail\n    let _ = len;\n    let mut repo = open(io);\n    // torn tail truncated; new writes continue cleanly\n    repo.append(\"s\", &points(8, 8)).unwrap();\n    repo.flush(\"s\").unwrap();\n    assert_eq!(repo.query(\"s\", 0, 1000).unwrap(), points(0, 16));\n}\n"},"name":"Write","type":"tool_use"}],"id":"msg_4436f0481fc6a9336c6f225c","model":"auto_model/urm","role":"assistant","stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"type":"message","usage":{"input_tokens":1630,"cache_creation_input_tokens":0,"cache_read_input_tokens":61440,"output_tokens":1478,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"},"stop_details":null},"type":"assistant","uuid":"052c7212-7fd2-46ae-92e8-63ee78ef6867","timestamp":"2026-09-22T16:20:55.244Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/admin/Downloads/biaozhul/opp7/a","sessionId":"7fd090d4-9561-42d2-9a61-8c78c8ab4a00","version":"2.1.197","gitBranch":"ab/run-20260922T151547Z/opp7-a","slug":"happy-greeting-reddy"}

{"parentUuid":"8e0d1b08-fa8b-4ecf-b825-ace0f99540eb","isSidechain":false,"message":{"content":[{"id":"call_b4c3a03cdb5c48df956f058b","input":{"file_path":"/home/admin/Downloads/biaozhul/opp7/a/README.md","content":"# tsblock — 时间序列块编码（纯后端）\n\nRust 实现的文件支持时间序列存储库：整数时间戳 + 整数值的块编码\n（delta-of-delta 时间、整数差分值）、块索引区间读取、可注入 I/O 层故障模拟、\n本地 HTTP 验证入口。**零外部依赖**（仅用标准库），无前端。\n\n## 构建与运行\n\n```bash\ncargo build --release\ncargo test                      # 全部自动化测试（27 个）\ncargo test --test acceptance -- --nocapture   # 验收测试，打印真实压缩比\n\n# 启动本地 HTTP 验证入口\n./target/release/tsblock --dir /tmp/tsblock-data --addr 127.0.0.1:8080\n# 可选：--buffer-late（乱序点进入单独缓冲而非拒绝）  --block-size N（默认 256）\n```\n\n## 磁盘格式\n\n每个 series 两个文件（series 名限制为 `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`，直接作为文件名）：\n\n```\ndata/<series>.tsb   块数据文件\n  [0..8)   文件头: magic \"TSB1\" | version u16 LE (=1) | reserved u16\n  随后为块序列，每块：\n    块头（48 字节）:\n      count    u32 LE   点数\n      ts_start i64 LE   首时间戳\n      ts_end   i64 LE   末时间戳\n      val_min  i64 LE\n      val_max  i64 LE\n      ts_len   u32 LE   时间戳载荷字节数\n      val_len  u32 LE   值载荷字节数\n      crc32    u32 LE   CRC-32/IEEE，覆盖 ts_payload ++ val_payload\n    ts_payload   delta-of-delta 编码（见下）\n    val_payload  整数差分编码（见下）\n\nlate/<series>.tsl   乱序缓冲日志（仅 Buffer 模式）\n  原始记录，每条 16 字节：ts i64 LE | val i64 LE，按追加顺序\n```\n\n块索引**不落盘**：`open` 时扫描全部块头并逐块校验 CRC 重建（见“同步边界”）。\n\n## 编码与溢出处理\n\n- 时间戳：`ts[0]` 原始 8 字节；`ts[1]` 存 delta；`ts[i≥2]` 存 delta-of-delta，\n  均以 zigzag varint 编码。\n- 值：`v[0]` 原始 8 字节；`v[i≥1]` 存与前值的差分，zigzag varint。\n- **溢出（显式处理）**：所有差分在 i128 中计算。差分超出 i64 范围、或恰好等于\n  `i64::MIN`（其 zigzag 像 u64::MAX 被保留）时，写入 ESCAPE 标记\n  （varint `u64::MAX`）+ 该点的**绝对值** 8 字节。解码端对称处理，从绝对值\n  重建游程 delta；任何会使重建值越出 i64 范围的数据一律判为损坏（返回错误），\n  不做回绕。\n\n## 同步边界（durability）\n\n- `append` 接受的点仅在内存中，直到 `flush`。\n- `flush` 把待写点编码成块、追加到数据文件，然后对数据文件（及非空的 late\n  日志）做 `sync`（fsync）。`flush` 返回 Ok 后这些点才持久。\n- 崩溃可能留下撕裂尾部（半个块头/半条载荷）。`open` 时逐块扫描：第一个\n  不完整、长度不可能或 CRC 校验失败的块即终止扫描，并把文件**截断**到该\n  偏移；之前的块全部有效并进入索引。late 日志长度不是 16 的倍数时截断到\n  16 字节边界。\n- `flush` 返回 I/O 错误后，内存状态可能与磁盘不一致；调用方应丢弃仓库并\n  重新 `open`（走恢复路径）再重试。故障注入测试演示了该流程。\n- 未 flush 的点对同进程查询可见，但重启即丢失（测试\n  `unflushed_points_visible_but_not_durable` 固定了此语义）。\n\n## 乱序语义\n\n同一 series 内时间戳必须严格递增。\n\n- `LateMode::Reject`（默认）：`ts <= 最后接受 ts` 的点被拒绝，\n  `ts == last` 记 `duplicate`，`ts < last` 记 `out_of_order`，逐点返回。\n- `LateMode::Buffer`：这类点追加到该 series 的 late 日志。查询时与主块\n  合并：时间戳冲突时**主流（按序）点优先**；late 日志内部冲突时**先追加者\n  优先**（first-write-wins）。late 点永不改写已落盘的块。\n\n## HTTP 接口（本地验证入口）\n\n```\nGET  /health\nPOST /ingest   {\"series\":\"cpu\",\"points\":[[ts,val],...]}\nPOST /flush    {\"series\":\"cpu\"}            （或 /flush?series=cpu）\nGET  /query?series=cpu&from=0&to=100       （闭区间）\nGET  /stats?series=cpu\n```\n\n### 请求样例（实际运行记录）\n\n```bash\n$ curl -s http://127.0.0.1:18080/health\n{\"ok\":true}\n\n$ curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:18080/ingest -d '{\"series\":\"cpu\",\"points\":[[1700000000,42],[1700000001,45],[1700000002,-7],[1700000003,0],[1700000004,9223372036854775807],[1700000004,99],[1700000002,5]]}'\n{\"series\":\"cpu\",\"accepted\":5,\"buffered\":0,\"rejected\":[{\"ts\":1700000004,\"val\":99,\"reason\":\"duplicate\"},{\"ts\":1700000002,\"val\":5,\"reason\":\"out_of_order\"}]}\n\n$ curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:18080/flush -d '{\"series\":\"cpu\"}'\n{\"series\":\"cpu\",\"flushed\":true}\n\n$ curl -s \"http://127.0.0.1:18080/query?series=cpu&from=1700000000&to=1700000004\"\n{\"series\":\"cpu\",\"from\":1700000000,\"to\":1700000004,\"count\":5,\"points\":[[1700000000,42],[1700000001,45],[1700000002,-7],[1700000003,0],[1700000004,9223372036854775807]]}\n\n$ curl -s \"http://127.0.0.1:18080/query?series=cpu&from=1700000002&to=1700000002\"\n{\"series\":\"cpu\",\"from\":1700000002,\"to\":1700000002,\"count\":1,\"points\":[[1700000002,-7]]}\n\n$ curl -s \"http://127.0.0.1:18080/stats?series=cpu\"\n{\"series\":\"cpu\",\"points\":5,\"pending\":0,\"blocks\":1,\"late_points\":0,\"raw_bytes\":80,\"file_bytes\":89,\"late_bytes\":0,\"compression_ratio\":0.8989}\n\n$ curl -s \"http://127.0.0.1:18080/query?series=cpu&from=9&to=1\"\n{\"error\":\"query failed: query range: from > to\"}\n```\n\n（5 个点的小块压缩比 <1 属正常：48 字节块头 + 8 字节文件头的固定开销\n只有在点数上量后才被摊薄，见下方实测。）\n\n## 验收测试设计\n\n`tests/acceptance.rs` 中每个查询结果都与**参考未压缩数组**\n（`Vec<(i64,i64)>` 按区间过滤）逐点比对，覆盖：\n\n- 边界查询：整块边界、跨块缝、块首/块末单点、范围外、空结果、`from>to` 报错；\n- 负值与过零序列、大幅正负摆动；\n- 重复时间戳与乱序点（Reject 与 Buffer 两种模式的语义）；\n- i64 极值：`i64::MIN`/`i64::MAX` 的值与时间戳、溢出 ESCAPE 路径；\n- 重开持久性：已 flush 数据保留、未 flush 数据丢失；\n- 真实压缩比统计（见下）。\n\n`tests/faults.rs` 用 `MockIO` 注入故障：sync 失败、append 失败、读失败、\n撕裂尾部截断恢复、CRC 损坏块剔除、late 日志撕裂恢复、恢复后继续写入。\n\n`tests/http_api.rs` 起真实服务器（临时端口）走完整 HTTP 流程。\n\n## 实测运行记录\n\n环境：Linux x86_64，rustc 1.96.0，零外部依赖。\n\n```\n$ cargo test\ntest result: ok. 10 passed; 0 failed   (codec/json 单元测试)\ntest tsblock — 时间序列块编码（纯后端）\n\nRust 实现的文件支持时间序列存储库：整数时间戳 + 整数值的块编码\n（delta-of-delta 时间、整数差分值）、块索引区间读取、可注入 I/O 层故障模拟、\n本地 HTTP 验证入口。**零外部依赖**（仅用标准库），无前端。\n\n## 构建与运行\n\n```bash\ncargo build --release\ncargo test                      # 全部自动化测试（27 个）\ncargo test --test acceptance -- --nocapture   # 验收测试，打印真实压缩比\n\n# 启动本地 HTTP 验证入口\n./target/release/tsblock --dir /tmp/tsblock-data --addr 127.0.0.1:8080\n# 可选：--buffer-late（乱序点进入单独缓冲而非拒绝）  --block-size N（默认 256）\n```\n\n## 磁盘格式\n\n每个 series 两个文件（series 名限制为 `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`，直接作为文件名）：\n\n```\ndata/<series>.tsb   块数据文件\n  [0..8)   文件头: magic \"TSB1\" | version u16 LE (=1) | reserved u16\n  随后为块序列，每块：\n    块头（48 字节）:\n      count    u32 LE   点数\n      ts_start i64 LE   首时间戳\n      ts_end   i64 LE   末时间戳\n      val_min  i64 LE\n      val_max  i64 LE\n      ts_len   u32 LE   时间戳载荷字节数\n      val_len  u32 LE   值载荷字节数\n      crc32    u32 LE   CRC-32/IEEE，覆盖 ts_payload ++ val_payload\n    ts_payload   delta-of-delta 编码（见下）\n    val_payload  整数差分编码（见下）\n\nlate/<series>.tsl   乱序缓冲日志（仅 Buffer 模式）\n  原始记录，每条 16 字节：ts i64 LE | val i64 LE，按追加顺序\n```\n\n块索引**不落盘**：`open` 时扫描全部块头并逐块校验 CRC 重建（见“同步边界”）。\n\n## 编码与溢出处理\n\n- 时间戳：`ts[0]` 原始 8 字节；`ts[1]` 存 delta；`ts[i≥2]` 存 delta-of-delta，\n  均以 zigzag varint 编码。\n- 值：`v[0]` 原始 8 字节；`v[i≥1]` 存与前值的差分，zigzag varint。\n- **溢出（显式处理）**：所有差分在 i128 中计算。差分超出 i64 范围、或恰好等于\n  `i64::MIN`（其 zigzag 像 u64::MAX 被保留）时，写入 ESCAPE 标记\n  （varint `u64::MAX`）+ 该点的**绝对值** 8 字节。解码端对称处理，从绝对值\n  重建游程 delta；任何会使重建值越出 i64 范围的数据一律判为损坏（返回错误），\n  不做回绕。\n\n## 同步边界（durability）\n\n- `append` 接受的点仅在内存中，直到 `flush`。\n- `flush` 把待写点编码成块、追加到数据文件，然后对数据文件（及非空的 late\n  日志）做 `sync`（fsync）。`flush` 返回 Ok 后这些点才持久。\n- 崩溃可能留下撕裂尾部（半个块头/半条载荷）。`open` 时逐块扫描：第一个\n  不完整、长度不可能或 CRC 校验失败的块即终止扫描，并把文件**截断**到该\n  偏移；之前的块全部有效并进入索引。late 日志长度不是 16 的倍数时截断到\n  16 字节边界。\n- `flush` 返回 I/O 错误后，内存状态可能与磁盘不一致；调用方应丢弃仓库并\n  重新 `open`（走恢复路径）再重试。故障注入测试演示了该流程。\n- 未 flush 的点对同进程查询可见，但重启即丢失（测试\n  `unflushed_points_visible_but_not_durable` 固定了此语义）。\n\n## 乱序语义\n\n同一 series 内时间戳必须严格递增。\n\n- `LateMode::Reject`（默认）：`ts <= 最后接受 ts` 的点被拒绝，\n  `ts == last` 记 `duplicate`，`ts < last` 记 `out_of_order`，逐点返回。\n- `LateMode::Buffer`：这类点追加到该 series 的 late 日志。查询时与主块\n  合并：时间戳冲突时**主流（按序）点优先**；late 日志内部冲突时**先追加者\n  优先**（first-write-wins）。late 点永不改写已落盘的块。\n\n## HTTP 接口（本地验证入口）\n\n```\nGET  /health\nPOST /ingest   {\"series\":\"cpu\",\"points\":[[ts,val],...]}\nPOST /flush    {\"series\":\"cpu\"}            （或 /flush?series=cpu）\nGET  /query?series=cpu&from=0&to=100       （闭区间）\nGET  /stats?series=cpu\n```\n\n### 请求样例（实际运行记录）\n\n```bash\n$ curl -s http://127.0.0.1:18080/health\n{\"ok\":true}\n\n$ curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:18080/ingest -d '{\"series\":\"cpu\",\"points\":[[1700000000,42],[1700000001,45],[1700000002,-7],[1700000003,0],[1700000004,9223372036854775807],[1700000004,99],[1700000002,5]]}'\n{\"series\":\"cpu\",\"accepted\":5,\"buffered\":0,\"rejected\":[{\"ts\":1700000004,\"val\":99,\"reason\":\"duplicate\"},{\"ts\":1700000002,\"val\":5,\"reason\":\"out_of_order\"}]}\n\n$ curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:18080/flush -d '{\"series\":\"cpu\"}'\n{\"series\":\"cpu\",\"flushed\":true}\n\n$ curl -s \"http://127.0.0.1:18080/query?series=cpu&from=1700000000&to=1700000004\"\n{\"series\":\"cpu\",\"from\":1700000000,\"to\":1700000004,\"count\":5,\"points\":[[1700000000,42],[1700000001,45],[1700000002,-7],[1700000003,0],[1700000004,9223372036854775807]]}\n\n$ curl -s \"http://127.0.0.1:18080/query?series=cpu&from=1700000002&to=1700000002\"\n{\"series\":\"cpu\",\"from\":1700000002,\"to\":1700000002,\"count\":1,\"points\":[[1700000002,-7]]}\n\n$ curl -s \"http://127.0.0.1:18080/stats?series=cpu\"\n{\"series\":\"cpu\",\"points\":5,\"pending\":0,\"blocks\":1,\"late_points\":0,\"raw_bytes\":80,\"file_bytes\":89,\"late_bytes\":0,\"compression_ratio\":0.8989}\n\n$ curl -s \"http://127.0.0.1:18080/query?series=cpu&from=9&to=1\"\n{\"error\":\"query failed: query range: from > to\"}\n```\n\n（5 个点的小块压缩比 <1 属正常：48 字节块头 + 8 字节文件头的固定开销\n只有在点数上量后才被摊薄，见下方实测。）\n\n## 验收测试设计\n\n`tests/acceptance.rs` 中每个查询结果都与**参考未压缩数组**\n（`Vec<(i64,i64)>` 按区间过滤）逐点比对，覆盖：\n\n- 边界查询：整块边界、跨块缝、块首/块末单点、范围外、空结果、`from>to` 报错；\n- 负值与过零序列、大幅正负摆动；\n- 重复时间戳与乱序点（Reject 与 Buffer 两种模式的语义）；\n- i64 极值：`i64::MIN`/`i64::MAX` 的值与时间戳、溢出 ESCAPE 路径；\n- 重开持久性：已 flush 数据保留、未 flush 数据丢失；\n- 真实压缩比统计（见下）。\n\n`tests/faults.rs` 用 `MockIO` 注入故障：sync 失败、append 失败、读失败、\n撕裂尾部截断恢复、CRC 损坏块剔除、late 日志撕裂恢复、恢复后继续写入。\n\n`tests/http_api.rs` 起真实服务器（临时端口）走完整 HTTP 流程。\n\n## 实测运行记录\n\n环境：Linux x86_64，rustc 1.96.0，零外部依赖。\n\n```\n$ cargo test\ntest result: ok. 10 passed; 0 failed   (codec/json 单元测试)\ntest
